Actual Answer

The Cloud Walker Walking Boot for Foot & Ankle Support is a full-shell orthopedic boot designed to support recovery from foot, ankle, and lower leg injuries.

Key features:
- Full Shell Protection: Rigid outer shell offers maximum support and stability for foot, ankle, and lower leg injuries.
- Adjustable Air Cell System: Built-in air chambers allow personalized compression for improved comfort and reduced swelling.
- Shock-Absorbing Sole: Cushioned sole helps minimize impact and promotes a more natural walking motion during recovery.
- Universal Fit: Can be worn on either the left or right foot, with adjustable straps for a secure, customized fit.
- Post-Operative & Injury Support: Intended for fractures, sprains, soft tissue injuries, and post-surgical recovery.

Product variants and codes:
- Cloud Walker | Walking Boot – Small: PRODUCT CODE CWBS
- Cloud Walker | Walking Boot – Medium: PRODUCT CODE CWBM
- Cloud Walker | Walking Boot – Large: PRODUCT CODE CWBL

Product description (all sizes):
The Cloud Walker Full Shell Ankle Boot is a durable, medical-grade orthopedic support device designed to aid in recovery from foot, ankle, or lower leg injuries. Its robust full-shell design provides maximum protection and stability for fractures, sprains, post-surgical healing, and soft tissue injuries. The adjustable air cell system enables customized compression and enhanced comfort while helping to reduce swelling. The lightweight, shock-absorbing sole provides smooth mobility, making it easier to walk without placing excess stress on the injured area. Actual Answer

Pain Management Technologies (PMT) describes its Soft-Touch single conductive TENS electrodes as premium, reusable gel pads designed for a wide range of electrotherapy devices.

Key construction and materials:
- Soft-Touch™ design with a soft cloth or blue fabric backing (non-porous tricot-style backing in the premium line)
- High-quality, latex-free conductive gel made in the USA
- High-quality carbon layer for even current dispersion
- Solid pigtail pin connector (standard 2 mm pin type) that fits most TENS, EMS, interferential (IF), Russian, microcurrent, and galvanic devices
- Oval corners to help minimize edge curl and improve adhesion

Performance and resistance:
- Approximately 45 ohms of resistance
- Designed to provide great dispersion of stimulation, good durability, and consistent comfort

Reusability and lifespan:
- Self-adhesive and reusable
- Typical lifespan is about 10–15 uses per pad (or more), depending on:
  - Length and intensity of each use
  - Skin condition
  - How well they are cared for and stored

Care and use tips from PMT:
- Apply pads only to clean, dry skin (no lotions or oils before use)
- After use, you can gently rub a small drop of water onto the gel surface to help restore moisture and extend pad life
- Always place electrodes back on the original backing sheet and store them in the resealable, air-tight pouch immediately after use
- Replace electrodes when the conductive gel starts to wear down, they lose stickiness, or stimulation becomes uneven
- Do not wash or soak the pads in water; that can damage the gel and reduce adhesion and conductivity

Compatibility:
- Labeled as “universal” for almost all TENS and EMS units, as well as IF and Russian stimulation units, that use standard pin-type connectors

Sizes and formats (examples from PMT’s Soft-Touch line):
- 2.0" x 2.0" square
- 2.0" round
- 3.0" round
- 1.5" x 4" rectangular
- Butterfly 6" x 3.3"
- Typically packaged as:
  - 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack for most shapes
  - 10 packs of 1 electrode per pack for butterfly style

If you tell me which exact single-conductive pad (size/shape or product code like FA2000 / FA3000 / FA3360) you’re interested in, I can narrow the details specifically to that model.

Actual Answer

Neck Traction Device by Air Collar - Neck Stretcher - Cervical Traction Device - Neck & Shoulder Pain Relief - Stretcher Collar for Improved Spine Alignment

The Air Collar neck traction device is designed to help with neck tension and related discomfort by gently stretching and supporting the cervical spine.

Key details from PMT:

• Neck tension relief: The Air Collar neck traction machine is designed to reduce pressure on your nerves by encouraging improved posture and stretching the neck. It can be used for stiff neck, pinched nerve, or sore shoulder muscles. Using it for just minutes a day may help increase flexibility and range of motion with at‑home neck traction.

• Fully adjustable traction: This cervical traction unit is easily adjustable. The precision knobs on the left and right sides can be moved up and down to reach the desired traction strength, up to 20 lbs of pressure. You can control the pressure of the back air‑bladder, which gently lifts the cervical spine using vertical air columns.

• Natural realignment: During use, spinal decompression allows muscles to stretch, helping free nerve root tissue and allowing vertebral discs to realign toward the natural 20‑degree curve of the cervical spine.

• Everyday relief support: Regular use can help relieve stress from standing, sitting, and office work over extended periods, which often leads to muscle tension, stiffness, and fatigue.

PMT also offers variations of this concept:
- 1st Generation Air Collar traction device
- 2nd Generation manual Air Collar traction device
- 2nd Generation Air Collar traction device with electric pump

These models share the same core purpose of adjustable cervical traction for neck and shoulder discomfort and posture support.

Note: This device is a traction collar, not a TENS (electrical stimulation) unit. It works through mechanical traction and support rather than electrical pulses. For electrical pain relief, a separate TENS unit would be used alongside or in addition to traction, if appropriate and cleared by your healthcare provider.

Actual Answer

EverTrac CT800 Neck Cervical Traction Device Home Unit System (Made in Taiwan)

The EverTrac CT800 is a home-use cervical traction device designed to relieve neck strain, pain, and muscle spasms and to support cervical spine health.

Key points:
- Product name: EverTrac CT800 Neck Cervical Traction Device Home Unit System
- Product code: CT800
- Intended use: At-home cervical traction for neck pain, neck strain, muscle spasm relief, and to help decompress the cervical spine.
- Technology: Uses Direct Cable Technology rather than air. This provides precise, controlled traction force and avoids issues related to air leakage.
- Traction force: Delivers traction up to 50 lbs.
- Operation: Traction force is adjusted with a traction knob; users typically reach the prescribed traction level with just 1–2 turns, allowing a quick and hassle-free setup.
- Therapeutic effect: Decompresses spinal structures and relaxes neck muscles to address musculoskeletal or neurological conditions affecting the cervical region.
- Usage setting: Designed for safe, convenient home use with professional-grade reliability, under the guidance of a doctor or health professional.
- Country of manufacture: Made in Taiwan.

This system is focused on mechanical cervical traction (not electrical stimulation like a TENS unit) and is intended as a professional-style traction solution adapted for home use.

• One fitted grounding bed sheet (Earth Sheets brand, conductive fabric as described above)

Actual Answer

Cervical Traction Device Neck Pain Relief by Theratrac – Regular

UNLOCKS NECK MUSCLES: Theratrac gently stretches neck muscles to slowly relax, allowing misaligned vertebrae to resume their normal supportive position.

REALIGNS DISCS: Vertebral discs realign with pneumatic decompression, freeing the nerve root tissue from the pressure of the discs.

PAIN RELIEF: With two points of traction, Theratrac provides pain relief, muscle relaxation and stretching, and posture improvement.

EASY TO USE: Inflate and deflate easily with hand pumps that inflate up to 30 lbs of pressure. Measure neck circumference to find your size.

PORTABLE: Durable, lightweight, convenient.

PRODUCT CODE: PCT100-R

DESCRIPTION: The Theratrac gently stretches neck muscles allowing the vertebral discs to realign, freeing the nerve root tissue from the pressure of the discs. The Theratrac helps “locked” neck muscles to slowly stretch and relax, allowing misaligned vertebrae to resume their normal supportive position. The Theratrac cradles the head and neck via a curvature in the neck portion of the device, as well as a special head rest section that is vertically pneumatic. Both the headrest and neck region inflate with over 30 lbs of adjustable pneumatic pressure to create traction in the cervical curve. Measure neck circumference to find your size.

Actual Answer

Hidden Heat Electric Foot of The Bed Warmer by Spotwarm is a microplush flannel mattress heating pad designed to sit at the foot of your bed, under the fitted sheet, so the heat source and cords stay out of sight.

Key details:

• Hidden heating pad placement:
  - Fits under the fitted sheet at the base of the bed.
  - Cords remain hidden and are controlled through a wireless RF remote.

• Comfort and sleep support:
  - Provides focused heat at the feet.
  - Heated feet help you fall asleep faster and stay in deep sleep for longer (supports REM).
  - Microplush flannel fabric allows the warmer to fit seamlessly under the sheet and adds softness.
  - You can also turn the warmer vertically for a full-body heat experience.

• Heat settings and safety:
  - 6 different heating levels so you can find your preferred warmth.
  - Each heat level has its own auto-shutoff feature.

• Ease of use:
  - 13 ft long power cord makes it easy to reach most outlets while staying hidden underneath the bed.

• Heating performance:
  - Quick-heating wire grid warms up faster than standard electric warmers.
  - Designed to retain heat for longer at the foot of the bed.

• Sizes and product code (Queen version):
  - Queen Bed pad size: 60" x 24" (works like an oversized heating pad for the bed).
  - Voltage: 110–120V.
  - Product code (Queen): SPQB10.

If you’d like, tell me whether you have a Queen or King bed and I can confirm the exact size and code for your version.

Actual Answer

Shoulder Rotator Cuff Stretching Device by Stretch Towel is a physical therapy shoulder and full body stretching strap designed to help improve shoulder flexibility and stretch sore or tight muscles.

Key points:

• Product name: Shoulder Rotator Cuff Stretching Device by Stretch Towel  
• Product code: STROT10  
• Category: Stretching Equipment / Massage Therapy Device  

Main features:
• Perfect shoulder stretch – specifically designed to provide a targeted stretch to the rotator cuff to help improve shoulder flexibility.
• Full body stretching – can also be used to perform a variety of other stretches for different body parts, not just the shoulder.
• Versatile fit – one-size-fits-all design, easily adjustable to fit all body types.
• Machine washable – can be machine washed; air dry is recommended for best use.

How it works (shoulder use concept):
The Stretch Towel is wrapped securely around the shoulder. As the user moves their arm forward, the towel tightens around the shoulder, creating a counter-lever and tension that drives the stretch into the rotator cuff area. This targeted mechanism is designed to provide an effective stretch where many other products cannot.

Summary of user steps for shoulder stretch:
1. Hang or drape the Stretch Towel over your neck with one side hanging lower than the other.
2. Take the longer end and wrap it back around the shoulder, grabbing the handle on that same side.
3. Once wrapped and positioned around the shoulder, you are ready to perform the stretch.
4. Pull downward on the handle with the opposite arm to create tension. With the arm on the wrapped-shoulder side, hold the handle horizontally and rotate the forearm downward/forward in roughly a 45° sweeping motion.
5. You should feel the towel grab and pull on the shoulder, producing the stretch.
6. Perform this stretch about 15 times, holding at the end of the motion to deepen the stretch.

Specification:
• Product weight: 1 lb (approx.)

This device is designed as a stretching aid, not as an electrical TENS unit.

Actual Answer

Wireless TENS Unit Stimulator by iTENS is an FDA‑cleared, over‑the‑counter, wireless TENS therapy device that pairs with a free iOS or Android app on your smartphone.

Key points from the PMT information:

• **Wireless and app‑controlled** – The iTENS connects to your smartphone or tablet via Bluetooth. All controls (intensity, mode, timer, body‑part selection) are managed through the app instead of on a handheld unit.

• **Revolutionary app design** – Described as the world’s first FDA‑cleared, OTC, wireless TENS device controlled by a smartphone app. Generation 2 devices are compatible with a wider range of phones and tablets.

• **Rotating ultra‑flexible wings** – This small red version uses second‑generation rotating wings that allow **360° movement** for full range of motion. The wings are thinner and more flexible for comfort and have a **laser‑printed silver conductive surface** for good conduction.

• **Customizable programs** – You can select treatment programs by **body part**, **pain condition**, or set up a **manual/custom program** in the app.

• **Drug‑free pain management** – Uses Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation (TENS) to send tiny electrical signals through the skin. These signals help block pain messages from reaching the brain and can help stimulate endorphin release, providing non‑medication pain relief for areas like back, knee, and other joints.

• **Progress tracking** – The app lets you **track your pain relief results before and after sessions**, so you can monitor how well specific settings or programs work for you.

If you tell me what you’d like to know next (for example: setup, where it’s typically used, or app features), I can focus on that part of the iTENS wireless unit.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch Silver Electrodes with tricot back (Tyco gel) are part of PMT’s premium reusable electrode line. They use a high-quality conductive silver layer and a soft tricot (cloth) backing combined with Tyco-brand gel for good adhesion, comfort, and consistent conductivity.

They are designed for use with TENS, EMS, interferential, microcurrent, and galvanic stimulators that accept standard pigtail pin connectors, and they are reusable when properly cared for (applied to clean, dry skin and stored on the liner in a sealed pouch between uses). The silver construction provides low impedance and even current dispersion, while the tricot cloth backing and Tyco gel focus on patient comfort and secure skin contact.

For sizing options and exact pack quantities of the Soft-Touch Silver tricot-back (Tyco gel) electrodes, refer to PMT’s Electrodes section under “Soft-Touch Silver Electrodes” on paintechnology.com.

Actual Answer

The Acupillow Neck Stretch Massage Trigger Point Chiropractic Pillow is a cervical traction and acupressure device designed for neck and shoulder tension relief.

Key features and information from PMT:

• **Acupressure pillow design** – The Acupillow is a firm (but not overly stiff) neck and shoulder relaxer with specially shaped neck supports that contact key acupressure points to help release neck tension.

• **Comfortably cradles the neck** – Unlike bulky cervical traction collars, this device is shaped like a supportive pillow. You simply rest your head and neck on it and relax on a firm surface, allowing it to cradle the neck comfortably while it works.

• **Neck curve corrector** – It is designed as a cranial/cervical cradle that helps restore the natural curve (S-curve) of the cervical spine. As you lie on it, gravity helps reposition cervical alignment into a more natural shape, providing gentle cervical traction and spinal alignment.

• **Intended benefits** – PMT describes benefits including:
  - Neck pain relief and neck traction
  - Shoulder pain relief
  - Stress relief and general relaxation
  - Helping alleviate tension, headaches, and migraines
  - Molding to the painful area for a more customized experience

• **Use and results** – Many users may begin to feel relief within about 5 minutes of use, and with continued daily use, it is intended to support more lasting neck alignment and reduced neck pain.

• **Where and how to use** –
  - Designed to be used on any firm surface (e.g., floor, firm mattress, or similar), including at home or while traveling (such as in a hotel room).
  - It is a therapeutic rest pillow/traction aid, **not** a sleeping pillow, and is not meant to be used overnight.

• **Construction and materials** – Made from high-quality, high-density foam that supports the neck and allows acupressure-style contact with cervical and shoulder regions.

• **Product details** –
  - Product name: Acupillow Acupressure Neck Pain Relief Cushion / Neck Stretch Massage Trigger Point Chiropractic Pillow
  - Product code: **AP100R**
  - Category: Massage Therapy Device / Cervical traction stretcher, acupressure pillow.

If you’d like, I can also explain how the Acupillow Pro (full back and neck version) compares to this standard Acupillow.

Actual Answer

Pain Management Technologies (PMT) offers several lines of Soft-Touch™ electrodes designed for TENS, EMS, interferential, microcurrent, galvanic and similar electrotherapy devices.

General Soft-Touch electrode features (all lines):
- Self-adhesive, reusable disposable electrodes
- High-quality, latex-free PMT gel
- Oval corners to help minimize edge curl
- Pig-tail style connector with a snug 2 mm pin plug
- Designed for good current dispersion and durability
- Packaged in resealable pouches to help keep pads fresh
- Compatible with most TENS/EMS/IF/Russian units that use standard pin-type or pigtail connectors

1) Soft-Touch Foam Electrodes (PMT gel)
- Backing: Foam (does not absorb sweat like cloth)
- Conductive layer: Ag silver conductor for strong performance
- Connector: Ultra-tight pigtail connector with snug plug, prewired
- Typical uses: TENS, EMS, Interferential, Microcurrent, Galvanic generators
- Reusable and value priced
- Common sizes & product codes:
  • 2.0" x 2.0" square, foam backing – PRODUCT CODE: FAF2020 – 10 packs of 4 electrodes/pack
  • 2.0" Round, foam backing – PRODUCT CODE: FAF2000 – 10 packs of 4 electrodes/pack

2) Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes – Cloth Back (PMT gel)
- Backing: Resilient blue fabric/cloth
- Resistance: About 45 ohms
- Reuse: Typically 10–15 or more uses per pad, depending on length of each use
- Connector: Pig-tail pin connector, universal for most TENS, EMS, IF, Russian units
- Packaging: Resealable pouch, generally 4 electrodes per pack
- Life-span tips:
  • After use, a small drop of water rubbed on the gel surface can help extend life
  • Always return pads to the airtight package directly after use
  • Replace electrodes when the conductive gel starts to wear down (often after ~10–15 uses)
- Product codes & sizes (examples):
  • FA2000 – 2.0" x 2.0" square or 2.0" round, 4 electrodes per pack, 10 packs per case
  • FA3000 – 2.0" x 2.0" square, 2.0" round, 3.0" round, or butterfly 6" x 3.3"; 4 electrodes per pack (butterfly: 1 per pack), 10 packs per case

3) Soft-Touch Medical Grade / Premium Electrodes
- Construction: Non-porous tricot or cloth backing, conductive wire-mount layer, high-quality carbon layer, extra-thick gel
- Connector: Ultra-snug pigtail connection
- Designed for: Broad compatibility across TENS, EMS, Interferential, Microcurrent, and Galvanic devices
- Goal: Enhanced comfort, contact, and durability with premium dispersion

Electrode selection & care basics from PMT:
- Do NOT wash or soak TENS electrode pads in water; this can damage the gel and reduce adhesion and performance.
- Longer-lasting performance depends on:
  • Proper storage in the resealable pouch immediately after use
  • Avoiding dirt, lotions, and oils on the skin before applying
  • Gentle handling when removing from skin and liner
- Typical use-life is around 10–15 uses per pad, then replace once gel is worn or adhesion/stimulation becomes inconsistent.

If you tell me what size/shape you need (2" x 2", round, butterfly, foam vs. cloth backing), I can narrow down to the exact PMT electrode product and code for you.

Actual Answer

Portable Neck Strengthener by NeckSpring (Product Code: NSpring10)

The NeckSpring is an innovative neck exerciser and posture corrector designed to target the underlying problem behind poor posture and “nerd neck.”

Key points:
- Helps combat “nerd neck” by relieving discomfort after spending long periods in front of screens.
- Intended to help restore cervical curvature and improve posture when used regularly as an exercise device.
- Minimalistic, easy-on/easy-off design so exercises can be performed in moments.
- Extremely lightweight and portable so it can be used at work, at home, or on the go.
- Coil guards help eliminate the risk of pinching.
- Padded back adds support for the neck and shoulders.
- Built with high-velocity springs that maintain powerful resistance and are designed not to lose strength even with consistent use.

The device is categorized under traction/exercise tools for neck posture and curvature support.

Actual Answer

The Toilet Tilt – Heavy-Duty, Adjustable Assist Seat for Seniors at Home or in Medical Settings is an electric toilet lift chair designed to make sitting down and standing up from the toilet safer and easier for people with mobility challenges.

Key features and information:

• Smooth glide motion: Uses a silent, stepless lifting mechanism to gently and steadily raise and lower the user. This helps provide stable support for a wide range of mobility levels.

• Spacious, heavy‑duty build: The seat and armrests are wide and ergonomically shaped, built on a high‑strength plastic frame designed to support up to 200 kg (about 440.92 lbs).

• Adjustable height / tilt: The lift offers an adjustable height range of 0–11 inches, allowing the seat position to be customized for comfort and easier sit‑to‑stand transitions.

• Rechargeable battery operation: Includes a built‑in rechargeable battery. The Toilet Tilt can be used while plugged in for continuous operation, or on battery power alone when unplugged. The unit will need to be recharged when the battery runs out.

• Backup power for reliability: A backup battery helps ensure the lift function continues to work during power outages, helping maintain safety and routine without interruption.

• Easy setup and custom fit: Designed for relatively simple assembly. The seat height is adjustable so it can be tailored to the user and bathroom setup. Additional wheels are available to make installation and repositioning easier where needed.

• Quiet, everyday use: The whisper‑quiet lift mechanism is intended to be discreet and comfortable for daily use.

Intended use:
This heavy‑duty Toilet Tilt is designed to support seniors and individuals with mobility challenges in both home and medical/healthcare environments. It focuses on comfort, stability, and independence during everyday bathroom routines.

Product code: TTLift

Actual Answer

The Ultima Combo from Pain Management Technologies is a dual-channel electrotherapy device that combines both TENS (Transcutaneous Electrical Nerve Stimulation) and EMS (Electrical Muscle Stimulation) functions in one unit.

Key points to know:

• **Device type & purpose**: It is PMT’s premier combo stimulator, combining the technology of the Ultima 20 TENS unit and the Ustim EMS unit. It is designed to provide versatile electrotherapy options for a wide range of needs.

• **Body part diagram interface**: The unit operates with a detailed, comprehensive digital body diagram, helping guide you in selecting treatment areas and settings.

• **Channels & output**:
  - Dual-channel device (can run two lead wires/sets of electrodes at once)
  - Maximum output: 130 mA (peak value) across a 500 Ohm load

• **Adjustable settings**:
  - Pulse width: 50–400 microseconds, adjustable in 50 microsecond steps
  - Pulse rate (frequency): 1–110 Hz, adjustable
  - Multiple waveform options:
    - Symmetrical bi-phasic rectangular
    - Asymmetrical bi-phasic rectangular
    - Positive uniphase rectangular
    - Negative uniphase rectangular
    - Reverse symmetrical bi-phasic rectangular
  - 34 available programs
  - Adjustable timer to tailor session duration

• **What’s included in the package** (typical kit contents):
  - Ultima Combo device
  - 4 reusable electrode pads
  - Lead wires
  - Batteries
  - User manual & electrode placement guide
  - Carrying case

• **Basic product specs**:
  - Product code: UCOMBO
  - Category: Electrotherapy Device (TENS/EMS combo)
  - Channel: Dual
  - Product weight: 0.75 lb
  - Approx. dimensions: 10 cm (L) x 18 cm (W) x 8 cm (H)
  - HCPCS code: E0720/E0745

The adjustable timer, pulse width, and frequency, combined with multiple programs and waveforms, allow the Ultima Combo to address a broad spectrum of electrotherapy needs. For full operating instructions, safety information, and electrode placement details, refer to the user manual and electrode placement guide included with the device, or visit paintechnology.com for additional resources.

• PRODUCT NAME & CODE:
  - ThermaWax Paraffin Wax Machine
  - Product Code: TWKit

• WHAT IT’S FOR:
  - At‑home paraffin wax heat therapy for hands, feet, and elbows.
  - Helps relieve dry skin, cracked heels, and joint stiffness while providing a relaxing, spa‑like treatment.

• KEY FEATURES:
  - Auto lid design for convenient, safer use.
  - Fast 20‑minute wax meltdown so it’s ready to use quickly.
  - Precision temperature control with three heating modes to help maintain a comfortable, consistent temperature during treatment.
  - Energy‑efficient heating system that provides consistent temperature control without excessive power consumption, making it more eco‑friendly and cost‑effective.
  - Spacious design to comfortably accommodate hands, feet, or elbows.
  - Ergonomic handle for easy portability and storage.
  - Non‑stick inner pot and smooth surfaces for simple, hassle‑free clean‑up.

• INCLUDED IN THE KIT:
  - Base ThermaWax unit
  - 8 wax refills (dual‑scented): 4 Peach and 4 Lavender
  - 1 pair cotton gloves
  - 1 pair cotton booties
  - PE (plastic) gloves
  - PE (plastic) booties
  - 1 brush
  - 1 spatula
  - Color user manual

• THERAPY TYPE:
  - Heat therapy using paraffin wax. It is not a TENS (electrical stimulation) unit; it uses heated wax for moist heat benefits rather than electrical pulses.

• GENERAL EXPERIENCE:
  - Designed to provide a luxurious, spa‑like, aromatic experience at home with Peach and Lavender scented wax options.
  - Cotton gloves and booties help retain heat during treatment; PE gloves/booties support hygiene and easier cleanup.

If you’d like, I can outline a simple step‑by‑step example of how a typical paraffin session with ThermaWax is performed, based on the kit components.

Actual Answer

For neck and cervical spine treatment, PMT does not use standard TENS "electrode pads" with cryotherapy systems. Instead, PMT offers dedicated **cold (and hot) water therapy pads/wraps** that connect to circulating water therapy units.

Here are the key PMT details relevant to your request:

1) Cervical Spine Cold Water Therapy Pad (for Cryotherapy Units)
- **Product type:** Water Therapy Pad / Wrap (not a gel TENS electrode)
- **Primary use:** Hot or cold water circulation around the **cervical spine / neck area** for acute and chronic conditions.
- **Compatibility:**
  - Compatible with **Arctic Ice Classic, ARS, Polar Vortex, and Polar Sport** units directly.
  - **Coupling Converter is required** for **Arctic Ice Clear** and some other manufacturers’ units.
- **Flow & coverage:**
  - Provides **continuous water therapy** that circumferentially covers and treats the neck/cervical area.
  - Designed to facilitate **optimal water flow for maximum efficiency**.
- **Connections:**
  - Uses **quick connect/disconnect couplings** that provide a **leak‑proof, easy‑to‑use connection**.
  - Includes an **insulation hose** for increased thermal efficiency (higher R‑value).
- **Securing the pad:**
  - **Includes a strap** to secure around the area of need (neck/cervical spine).
- **Use with hot or cold:**
  - Designed for use with **hot or cold water therapy units** (cryotherapy or heat therapy).
- **Product code:** **TPC**
- **Specifications:**
  - Product weight: **1 lb**
  - HCPCS Code: **E0249**

2) Coupling Converters & Couplings (for compatibility with pads)
If you are connecting PMT water therapy pads (including the cervical pad) to various cryotherapy units, PMT provides **Water Therapy Coupling Attachments**:

- **Cold Water Therapy Coupling Converter**
  - **Product code:** **TP2020-CC**
  - Function: Changes a **standard, incompatible water therapy system** so it can use **PMT brand water therapy pads**.
  - Designed for both **hot and cold** water therapy units.
  - Intended as an **accessory item only** (does *not* include a cryotherapy machine or pad).
  - Uses **quick‑connect‑disconnect couplings** for **better, less‑leaky connections** than many competitor fittings.

- **Cold Water Therapy Couplings for Arctic Ice Clear**
  - **Product code:** **TP2020-CC-White**
  - Specifically used to convert/replace fittings on **Arctic Ice Clear, Arctic Medical, Coolman, and other water therapy units** so they can connect to **PMT water therapy pads**.
  - Also uses quick‑connect‑disconnect couplings to help **fix leaks** and provide secure attachment.

3) How this relates to TENS & “electrodes”
- PMT’s **cold water therapy pads for the neck/spine are not TENS gel electrodes**; they are **circulating water wraps/pads** that deliver hot or cold therapy through tubing.
- If you are using a separate **TENS unit** for electrotherapy, that would typically use **standard TENS electrode pads** (gel pads) placed on the skin, which are different products from these **water therapy pads**.

If you can clarify whether you need:
- a **cervical water therapy pad** for a cryotherapy device, or
- **TENS gel electrodes** sized/placed for neck or spine,
I can narrow this down further to the exact PMT accessory that fits your setup.

Actual Answer

Economy Edition Office Desk Chair Wrap by SnuggleBack – Key Information from PMT:

• Product type: Heat therapy chair blanket/wrap that attaches to an office chair for convenient, hands‑free warmth.

• Materials & Feel:
  - Premium fleece that is super soft and cozy.
  - Thinner and more nimble than faux fur – ideal for summer use when AC is blasting, yet still warm enough for cold winter mornings.
  - Sherpa fur chair blanket interior – a hybrid material mixing sherpa and fleece to create a fur‑like feel with sherpa‑like warmth that heats the body comfortably.

• Design & Features:
  - Patent pending, one‑of‑a‑kind chair blanket that attaches to any office chair.
  - Wrap the flaps together to create easy‑in and easy‑out convenience.
  - Mounts to the chair back to stay fixed in position and provide a “ready when you are” swaddle of cozy warmth.
  - Hands‑free: once attached, you simply sit and wrap/unwrap as desired.
  - Eco‑friendly: helps you stay warm without turning up the thermostat or using space heaters.
  - No‑risk heat: marketed as a safer way to stay warm in the office vs. dangerous space heaters.
  - Perfect size – one size fits most.

• Color Options in the Economy Edition Line:
  - Black faux fur with sherpa interior.
  - Grey fleece version (also listed with the same SnuggleBack office chair wrap description).

• How to Use (Instructions from PMT):
  1. Attach to the chair back by securing the straps in place.
  2. Drape the blanket over the armrests.
  3. Wrap and unwrap as desired while seated.

• Care Instructions:
  - Machine washable: wash cold with like colors.
  - Air dry.

• Specifications:
  - Product weight: 1 lb
  - Color example: Black (Black faux) or Grey (depending on model variant).
  - Product code example noted: Blackfau (for the black faux fur version).

• Intended Use / Setting:
  - For home office or work office.
  - Designed to keep you cozy in notoriously cold offices in winter or in summer when AC is blasting.

This summarizes the information PMT provides about the Economy Edition Office Desk Chair Wrap by SnuggleBack that attaches to your chair for convenient, hands‑free warmth.

- Product name: Neuro Ground Cuff
- Product code: ECuff

- Product weight: 2 lbs (listed as 2 in the specifications)
- HCPCS code: E0731

It is categorized under Electrotherapy Garments / Neuro Ground Cuff in the PMT product lineup.
